 Data Connect 2026.1.2 introduces the following internal code changes:
- Updated metadata to prepare for future enhancements. 
 | Developer Center: Enhancements and Updates in Data Connect 2026.1.4 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.4
 | Enhancement (updated functionality) 
 | N/A
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 Function/Module 
 Description 
 | R Library - Get Datasets 
 | 
- Retrieve datasets in study environments without requiring study UUID in input attributes. 
- Dataset pagination lets you use Page Size and Page Number to control which results return in a single request. 
 | R Library - Get Dataset Versions 
 | 
- Retrieve datasets in study environments without requiring study or study environment UUIDs in input attributes. 
 | R Library - Get Studies 
 | 
- User with the Manage Custom Code projects permissions can retrieve a list of available studies and study environments. 
- Study pagination lets you use Page Size and Page Number to specify which studies return in a single request. 
 | R Library - Fetch Data 
 | Retrieve datasets in study environments without requiring study or study environment UUIDs in input attributes. 
 | R Library - DateTime Format support 
 | 
- Specify the date and date time format for a column in the data frames you publish. 
- R Library now validates your date and dat etime formats. 
- Data validation ensures that data transformed from R back into Data Connect matches the required date or datetime format. 
- Non-valid data records no longer publish. 
- You can now view the following errors: 
- Non valid date/datetime format column errors. 
-   Records where data does not match the defined format. 
 | R Library - Error Handling Framework 
 | A new error handling framework now governs all R functions, ensuring consistent failure reporting through standardized codes and actionable details.
 | Developer Center 
 | You can now filter and select the latest R library version in Developer Center Documentation page.  
 | Transformations: Internal and Technical Changes in Data Connect 2026.1.4 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.4 
 | Enhancement (updated functionality) 
 | None.
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The Transformations interface now dynamically adjusts width based on the available screen area. 
 | Imports: Support for Versionless Veeva EDC Datasets 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality)
 | Imports > Veeva EDC Direct Connect
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 Data Connect now uses a version-independent approach to import Veeva EDC data collected across multiple versions at a given time . Imported datasets now appear in a single table featuring a "superset" schema that contains all unique columns from every version of that form . 
 In addition, the system now creates consolidated visits that represent unique visits across all CRF versions found in the event_definitions file
 | Transformations: Quality of Life Enhancements in Data Connect 2026.1.0 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality) 
 | Transformations > Derived Datasets
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The R02 release introduces several usability enhancements designed to improve project management, data visibility, and transformation capabilities. 
- Archived Dataset Awareness: Data Connect now provides visual indicators and warnings when you interact with archived datasets. If a project uses a dataset from an archived project, the system shows a warning about potential state data.
- Archive Impact Analysis: Before you archive a project, a confirmation message details the datasets and downstream processes impacted by the action. In addition, archiving a project that saved as a template prevents future use of that template. 
- Project Filtering: You can now filter the project list to view only those projects created from a template. 
- Substring Transformation: You can now extract a substring using a self-defined delimiter during data transformation. 
 | Transformations: Project Capacity Limits for Derived Datasets 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Feature (New Functionality)
 | Transformations > Derived Datasets
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 Data Connect now includes a Project Capacity progress bar within Derived Datasets projects. This visual indicator shows the total execution time consumed by the project's operations against a hard limit of 60 seconds. 
 The system calculates capacity usage as you build a derived dataset on the canvas . The progress bar provides visual feedback using the following color indicators: 
- Green : 0% to 74% capacity 
- Yellow : 75% to 90% capacity 
- Red/Orange : 91% to 100% capacity 
 | Transformations: Support for Versionless Veeva EDC Datasets 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Feature (New Functionality)
 | Datasets
 Transformations
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 Data Connect now supports a versionless schema for Veeva data sources, consolidating multiple dataset versions into a single view. 
 While in Datasets, when previewing or profiling Veeva datasets, an All option is now available in the version list and is selected by default. 
 In addition, while in Transformations, the system shows an All badge next to  versionless folders in the Data Source panel, and nodes in the canvas lineage to indicate the dataset type. 
 | Developer Center: Developer Center and Data Source Enhancements 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality)
 | Data Connect > Developer Center
 | 
 Important : The R SDK and Developer Center are only available as a limited release for selected users.
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The R02 release introduces stablity and usability improvements to the Developer Center, alongside significant updates to Veeva data handling and dataset metadata retrieval. 
 Developer Center Updates 
- Token Management Safety: The Developer Center now show a confirmation page when you attempt to disable or regenerate an authentication token. You must confirm the action to proceed, preventing accidental deactivation of access keys. 
 Get Datasets Function Enhancements 
 The datasets() function result now includes the following metadata attributes for each dataset:
- Dataset Name 
- Dataset Short Name 
- List of Collections (optional) 
- Dataset Type, for example: Derived Dataset, Rave, SFTP Import, Custom Code 
- Dataset Source Name 
- Activation Status (optional) 
- Dataset Status 
- Last Updated (optional) 
 In addition, when a Veeva form contains data collected across different CRF versions, the  Get Datasets and Dataset Version functions now list all CRF versions as a comma-separated list. 
 | Transformations: Internal and Technical Changes in Data Connect 2026.1.0 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Technical Change
 | None.
 | 
 Data Connect routinely includes internal and technical changes to improve performance and optimize back-end processes. Data Connect 2026.1.0 introduces the following internal code changes to Datasets and Transformations:
- Infrastructure and Build Optimization 
- Backend Refactoring 
- UI Code Optimization 
 | Transformations: Enhanced String Transformations for Derived Variables 
 | Data Connect
 | 2026.1.0
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality)
 | Transformations > Derived Datasets
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The R02 release introduces enhancements to string transformation functions used in derived datasets. 
- Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls): The Simple Concatenate transformation is now called Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls) . This function now automatically skips null values during concatenation, ensuring that the specified delimiter does not repeat when data points are missing. 
- Substring with Delimiter: The Substring transformation now includes the ability to define a specific delimiter, allowing you to extract substrings based on character patterns rather than just fixed positions.

===== PAGE: data_connect_releases_history.html | Data Connect Releases History =====
## Data Connect Releases History
 The following lists all Data Connect releases (up to and including the current release) and the features or enhancements introduced in each.
 Important : All Data Connect Module Release Information Now Covered in One Data Connect Release Notes 
 Prior to the Data Connect June 2024 release, Datasets, Transformations, Imports, and Exports were each released as separate products with their own sets of release notes. As of Jun-26-2024, all release notes content for all Data modules—that is, Datahub, Transformations, Imports, and Exports—will be documented under one Data Connect Release Notes document per release beginning with the Data Connect June 2024 Feature Release Notes.  To view released features prior to the Data Connect 2024, see: 
- Datasets and Transformations Releases History 
- Imports Releases History 
- Exports Releases History 
 Code Continuity Across Releases
 All Data Connect releases are cumulative. That is, each release or software update builds on prior releases as noted in the release notes. For example, Software Update 2020.2.1 includes all release 2020.2.0 code, which in turn is based on the release 2020.1.0 code and so on. The release notes describe any updates between releases or software updates (urgent or otherwise).

===== PAGE: data_connect_roles_and_capabilities.html | Data Connect Roles and Capabilities =====
## Data Connect Roles and Capabilities
 To grant yourself or other users permission to use Data Connect, you must use Medidata Cloud Administration® to: 
- Add and edit roles . 
- Configure roles and provide levels of permission to Data Connect . 
- Assign roles to individuals, and remove roles throughout the client. 
 Important : When assigning users to a role, you must assign users to the study level. 
 You must have the necessary building blocks assigned to configure roles. Every building block includes one or more permissions to define the Data Connect capabilities listed in this article. In addition, you can only see data for the environments you have permission to use.
## Minimum Permission Requirements
 Module Combination 
 Requirements 
 | 
- Datasets 
- Imports 
- Exports 
- Transformations 
 | 
- Manage Data Sources 
- Manage Extracts 
- Extract Transformed Data 
- Manage Transformation Projects 
 | Exports only 
 | 
- Manage Extracts 
 Note : For details about other capabilities and experiences you can access, see the individual permissions by experience articles.
 Important : Studies on Forms Clinical that use the classic  Ingestor  experience and only need Data Connect's Exports capabilities can access Data Connect with the following building block combination: 
- Keep your current building blocks selected 
- Select Manage Extract Data 
- Do not select Extract Transformations Projects 
## Data Connect Building Blocks and Permissions
 The building blocks granted by client administrators to roles in Cloud Administration help influence the content available on a Data Connect page.
 Important :
- Assigning these building blocks to  roles in Cloud Administration is at the discretion of every customer. 
- When you add a building block to an existing role, the role does not automatically work with the new associated permissions. You must remove the role from the user and re-add the role to apply the permissions. 
 A role is assigned building blocks and all the permissions within those blocks. However, access to the permissions and the Data Connect pages associated with them is controlled by each user's context level assignment in Cloud Administration.
 The following table lists the building blocks and corresponding permissions for various operations within Data Connect.
 Important : To export data with the Rave and Audit CTMS data types for a given study, you must also use Cloud Administration's Manage Access tools to set Site and Country Access to all sites and countries.
 Datasets 
 Name 
 Permissions 
 | Manage Data Collections 
 | 
- Grants the ability to:
- Create, edit, and delete collections 
- Add and remove datasets from collections 
 Transformations 
 Name 
 Permissions 
 | Manage Transformation Projects 
 | 
- Adds the Data Connect application to your list of registered applications 
- Grants the ability to: 
- Access the Datasets experience 
- Access the Transformations experience. 
- Create and manage transformation projects, including : 
- SDTM-like datasets (in limited preview for select customers) 
- Custom data models (in limited preview for select customers) 
- Derived data models 
- Enriched views such as unit conversions 
- Write to the Data Connect data catalog 
 | Manage Library Templates 
 | 
- Grants the ability to create and edit derived dataset templates 
 | View Library Templates 
 | 
- Grants the ability to view and use derived dataset templates 
 | Manage Transformation Projects 
 | 
- Grants the ability to archive your own projects. Medidata recommends this setting for most users. 
 | Transformation Admin 
 | 
- Grants the ability to archive any user's project in the study group 
 Imports 
 Name 
 Permissions 
 | Manage Data Sources 
 | 
- Adds the Data Connect application to your list of registered applications   
- Grants the ability to: 
- Access to the Imports experience 
- Write to the Data Connect data catalog 
- See data sources and datasets added to Imports 
- Add data sources and datasets to Imports 
 Exports 
 Name 
 Permissions 
 | Manage Extracts 
 | 
- Adds the Data Connect application to your list of registered applications 
- Grants the ability to: 
- Access the Exports experience 
- Create exports, including: 
- Read workflow plans 
- Edit workflow plans 
- Create workflow plans 
- Execute workflow plans 
- Cancel workflow plans 
- Read clients 
- Read study environments 
- Read studies 
- Edit exports 
- Run exports 
 | View Unblinded Data 
 | 
- Grants access to unblinding study data 
 | Extract EDC Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract Rave clinical data 
 Note : If you assign this role, you must also:
- Assign the Unblinded User building block; and, 
- Select the All Sites and Countries flag. 
 | Extract CTMS Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract CTMS data 
 | Extract Master Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract Master data 
 | Extract Cloud Admin Audit Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract Cloud Admin Audit Master data 
 | Extract Rave CTMS Audit Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract CTMS Audit data 
 | Extract Rave Audit Data 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract Rave Audit data 
 | Extract Transformed Data 
 | You can extract Transformed data created through Transformations .
 | View as Super User 
 | 
- Adds the Data Connect application to your list of registered applications 
- Grants super user access, which allows you to view all exports within the client across the studies you have access to. 
 Note : Visible exports include both exports that you created, and exports other users created.
 | Extract Rave Companion Audit 
 | 
- Grants the ability to extract Rave Companion Audit data 
 .
 | Extractor Admin 
 | 
- Adds the Data Connect application to your list of registered applications 
- Grants the ability to: 
- Configure external connections as for extracted data, including: 
- SFTP 
- Amazon S3 
- FTPS 
- Manage connections 
 Note : You must assign this at the study group level because connections are available to use with all studies in the study group.

===== PAGE: data_lake_house_integration_with_extractor.html | Data Lake House Integration with Extractor =====
## Data Lake House Integration with Extractor
## What Is New?
 Platform Workbench includes access to Extractor capabilities. Extractor is Medidata's data export capability. 
 With Extractor, you can create export jobs to download data from the Medidata platform, including the new Transformed Data export type that export data set projects you create with Platform Workbench. Moreover, you can use Extractor functionality from Platform Workbench interface, including job administration, scheduling, and delivery configuration. 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 No impact to the workflow process. This is a new capability.
## Other Applications
 You can also create the Transformed Data export job type in the stand-alone Extractor interface.
## How Do I Benefit?
 You can export published data pertaining to data sets and transformations you create in Platform Workbench through an integrated export experience. 
## Where Do I See the Change?
 Platform Workbench now includes the Exports tab where you can create and manage export jobs, including the new Transformed Data export job. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Use the following links to learn more about Extractor's capabilities:
- Extracting Data with Export Jobs 
- Configuring Jobs to Deliver to SFTP 
- Configuring Jobs to Deliver to FTPS 
- Configuring Jobs to Deliver to Amazon S3 Locations 
- Extractor Data Objects 
 Articles specific to Platform Workbench will come at a future date.
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: data_lake_house_integration_with_sync.html | Data Lake House Integration with Sync =====
## Data Lake House Integration with Sync
## What Is New?
 Platform Workbench includes access to Sync capabilities. Sync is Medidata's new third-party data source import capability. With Sync, you can: 
- Configure data source connections for a study environment to import data through SFTP.  
- Configure data import criteria for Sync to identify and use for data transfer. 
- View a given import's progress and the status of the imported fields. 
 The Medidata platform includes all data sources uploaded with Sync, including non-Medidata data sources, in its lake house. Platform Workbench presents these data sources as data sets, you can perform data transformations on and across these data sources.
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 No impact to the workflow process. This is a new capability.
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications.
## How Do I Benefit?
 You can import non-Medidata data sources for your data projects through an integrated import experience. 
## Where Do I See the Change?
 Platform Workbench includes the Import tab where you can access Sync's capabilities. You can also access Sync's capabilities from by Datasets tab by clicking Add Dataset > Import. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Use the following links to learn more about Sync's capabilities:
- Sync Release Notes 
 Articles specific to Platform Workbench will come at a future date.
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: dataset_latency.html | Dataset Latency =====
## Dataset Latency
 Because the data can come into Data Connect from different sources, they can have different refresh cycles. This time also considers how long it takes for the record count to stabilize, that is: for the number of records that appear in the dataset to be consistent with the expected number of records.
 For example, a Rave dataset with 22M records can take up to 12 hours to appear and stabilize after loading them the first time.
 The table below lists the supported datasets and their data loading times.
 Dataset Type 
 Data Latency  
 Notes 
 | Rave – First Time Load / Metadata Change 
 | Up to 24 hours 
 | N/A 
 | Rave – Subsequent Updates 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A 
 | Raw External Datasets 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A 
 | Derived Datasets – First Activation 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A 
 | Derived Datasets – After Dataset Upgrade 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | This does not prevent you from using the full dataset with Transformations. 
 | Unit-converted Datasets 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A 
 | SDTM-like Datasets 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A 
 | Custom Data Models 
 | Up to 1 hour 
 | N/A

===== PAGE: dataset_profiling.html | Dataset Profiling =====
## Dataset Profiling
 Data Connect's Profiling capability examines and summarizes source data to review clinical datasets. With this feature, you can understand data structure, content, quality, and relationships. Profiling uncovers patterns, anomalies, and issues, such as missing values. It uses statistics and rules to create a data overview. Use this overview to assess data fitness for analytics, data warehousing, or integration. Profiling supports data governance and quality improvement.
- Access Data Connect , and  click   Datasets   in the left-hand menu. 
- Select a dataset by clicking its name on the left side of its row.   
- Select the Profiling  tab. 
- Select the Version of the dataset from the drop down list. 
- Select Fields to compare using the  Select fields list on the right. 
- Click +New Filter to add filters to the selected fields. 
- Click Compare  on the right-hand side to compare a new dataset profile. This creates a profile on the right-hand side. 
- Repeat the steps 4 and 5 to add fields and filters for the new dataset to compare. You can now compare two data profiles side by side.

===== PAGE: dataset_statuses.html | Dataset Statuses =====
## Dataset Statuses
 Data Connect - Datasets centralizes dataset statuses, allowing you to monitor the end-to-end health of your data from import through transformation and activation to export. This reference describes the status alerts, error reconciliation, and interface elements visible on the Datasets page. 
## Overview 
 The Datasets page includes indicators that help you identify and prioritize data issues: 
- The Activation column highlights a dataset's activation status. 
- The Status column consolidates errors from across Data Connect modules into a single location. Instead of checking individual modules separately for errors, you can view the reconciled status of a dataset directly. 
## Datasets Page Elements
 The  Datasets page includes indicators that help you identify and prioritize data issues. 
 Element 
 Description 
 | Activation 
 | Indicates the dataset's activation status. 
 You can filter on each status to see the counts and specific datasets pertaining to that status. 
 | Status 
 | Indicates the health of specific datasets within the list. Statuses appear as " Warning", with an orange triangle; or " Error", with a red circle. 
 You can filter on each status to see the counts and specific datasets currently triggering an alert. With this, you can quickly assess the volume of errors requiring attention. 
## Activation Status Types and Descriptions 
 Activation statuses reflect the state of an activated dataset. You can review details about the activation by clicking the dataset's name on the left of the row to see its details, and going to the Activations tab. 
 Status 
 Icon 
 Description 
 | Blanks 
 | N/A 
 | The dataset's initial state.
 | In Progress 
 | Gray Cycle Icon 
 | An interim state between Blank and Activated. Most datasets complete activation within five minutes. If a dataset remains in this state for more than one hour, contact Medidata Support. 
 | Activated 
 | Green Checkmark
 | The dataset is available for use in downstream applications such as Data Surveillance and RBQM.
 For example, in Data Surveillance and RBQM:
- You can see the data in the Data Surveillance and RBQM workflow after its next refresh cycle. 
- When you activate a new version of the dataset for Data Surveillance,  its configurations and review history can reset in Data Surveillance . 
- Any records associated with identified unregistered values are unavailable for the downstream applications that you activated the dataset for. 
 | Upgrade Available 
 | Orange Triangle 
 | The dataset has source datasets that have updated versions. You can upgrade the dataset to use the latest version. For details, see  Upgrade Datasets to Latest Version 
 | Inactive 
 | Grey Pause Icon 
 | The activated dataset is now paused.
 | Error 
 | Red Exclamation 
 | An issue occurred during processing.
## Dataset Status Types and Descriptions 
 Dataset statuses roll up from Imports, Exports, and Transformations. Selecting a specific dataset opens a details panel that isolates the issue to the specific stage in the pipeline. 
 Status 
 Icon 
 Description 
 Examples 
 | No issues 
 | N/A 
 | The dataset processed correctly across all modules. 
 | There are no issues with this dataset. 
 | Warning 
 | Orange Triangle 
 | The process is completed, but with issues, or a pipeline is inactive. 
 | Imports: Records Partially Imported 
 Transformations: Inactive pipeline 
 | Error 
 | Red Exclamation 
 | A critical issue occurred during processing. 
 | Imports: System processing error 
 Exports: Export 'Export_Name' has failed

===== PAGE: datasets.html | Datasets =====
## Datasets
 Data Connect's Datasets capability lists all available datasets that you have permission to see. You can see each dataset's label, type, source, activation status, and more. 
## What Is Datasets?
 Datasets is a table that shows you: 
- All datasets you have access to. 
- Metadata pertaining to each dataset, including: Dataset type, the upstream data source, activation status, and last refresh date. 
- Filtered views for the datasets, including: 
- Internal sources from within the Medidata platform, such as Rave data. 
- External sources imported through Data Connect, such as Labs data. 
- Transformed datasets produced through Data Connect's Transformations capability. 
## What Is the Purpose of Datasets?
 Datasets allows you to review the statuses of datasets those in your study group are using. You can see metadata about each dataset, including if a dataset is activated. You can use activated datasets for other capabilities compatible with Data Connect, such as Data Surveillance and RBQM. Therefore you quickly check datasets for further analysis as necessary. 
## Who Uses Datasets?
 Data managers, clinical data scientists, biostatisticians, data scientists, and programmers.
## How Does Datasets Work?
 Datasets lists the datasets available within your study group. Each listing includes the following details: 
- The dataset's name; version; and data type, such as if it is an Rave dataset or a derived datasets. 
- The dataset's data source details, such as if the Rave dataset comes from a Rave source. 
- The dataset's activation date and most recent refresh date. 
 Important : Metadata updates from Rave sources can take up to 24 hours to update in Data Connect.
 You can filter the table by dataset category, including: 
- Internal sources from within the Medidata platform, such as Rave data. 
- External sources imported through Data Connect, such as Labs data. 
- Transformed datasets produced through Data Connect's Transformations capability. 
 You can also create datasets from the Datasets screen, which serves as another link into Data Connect's Transformations capability. 
 Important :  Unsupported Data 
 Data Connect does not support variables such as coding dictionaries, coded terms, and code lists. While you can import datasets that includes these data, you cannot see or use them in Data Connect. However, these data can appear as-is in RBQM and Data Surveillance when you activate the associated dataset. For details, see Unsupported Variables in Data Connect . 
## What Are the Benefits of Datasets?
 Datasets provides a central location to: 
- Review datasets in your study group. 
- Check activated datasets that compatible capabilities are using, such as Data Surveillance. 
- Ensure that activated datasets are using the latest and greatest data configurations. 
## How Do I Access Datasets?
 You land on the Datasets dashboard when entering Data Connect. You can also reach the dashboard by clicking the Datasets tab while in Data Connect.

## Datasets: Organize Datasets with Dataset Collections
## What Is New?
 Users can now organize datasets in dataset Collections. With this feature you can:
- Create or remove a Collection for the study environment. 
- Add a dataset to one or more collections. 
- Browse or filter collections to view all the dataset that belongs to the collection. 
- Remove a dataset from the collection. 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All. 
## Workflow Process
 No change in workflow process.
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications
## How Do I Benefit?
 With Collections, you can organize the datasets in a logical manner. The Collections also support Tabular Visualizations in Data Surveillance. 
## Where Do I See the Change?
 To access this feature, access Data Connect and click Datasets on the left-hand menu.
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Use the following links to find new and updated content in support of this Feature: 
- Manage Dataset Collections 
## What Else Should I Know?
- You need access to building block Manage Data Connect Collections to: 
- Create or modify a Collection 
- Add a dataset to a Collection 
 Note : Without the access to building block, you can see the Collection in the datagrid but cannot make changes related to Collections.
- You cannot add Rave datasets to Collections yet due to the data catalog registration structure. For Rave datasets, the action menu item Add to Collection appears disabled in the data grid.

===== PAGE: datasets_and_transformations_releases_history.html | Datasets and Transformations Releases History =====
## Datasets and Transformations Releases History
 The following lists all releases for Data Connect's Datasets and Transformations capabilities before the June 2024 release, and the features or enhancements introduced in each.
 Important : As of the 2024.1.0 release, Platform Workbench is a part of the Data Connect solution as its "Datasets and Transformations" capability. It is now "Data Connect - Datasets and Transformations" or "Datasets and Transformations." For details what the 2024.1.0 release entails, and following releases, see   Data Connect Releases History , 
 Code Continuity Across Releases
 All Platform Workbench releases are cumulative.   That is, each release or software update builds on prior releases as noted in the release notes. For example, the  Datasets and Transformations Standard Software Update 2023.1.1 includes all release Datasets and Transformations Feature Release 2023.1.0 code, which in turn is based on the release  2022.1.0 code and so on. The release notes describe any updates between feature releases, standard s oftware updates, or other types of releases.

- Unit Conversion Projects Now Available
- Custom Target Generation Projects Now Available
- Integration with Sync
- Integration with Extractor
- Filtered Views During Field-Variable Mapping
- Internal and Technical Changes in Platform Workbench for 2023.4.0
## Platform Workbench 2023.3.0 Beta (Aug 2023)
- Transformations Now Include Multiple Concatenation
- Ability Delete Custom Transformations Now Available
- Ability to Delete Manually Entered Records for Selected Domains
- Internal and Technical Changes in Platform Workbench 2023.3.0
- Resolved Issues

- Internal Technical Changes

- Internal and Technical Changes

===== PAGE: date_time_variable_formats_supported.html | Date Time Variable Formats Supported =====
## Date Time Variable Formats Supported
 Date time variables you create can be used in various capabilities within Data Surveillance, RBQM, and Data Connect. Date variables include
 several date format variations that contain or omit the time. Key date parts include: year, month, day; and for time: hour, minute, seconds, and milliseconds. Data Surveillance, RBQM, and Data Connect support the following datetime formats: 
 | MMM- dd- yyyy 
 | dd/mm/yyyy HH:nn:ss 
 | yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss 
 | yyyy- mm- dd- HH:nn:ss 
 | yyyy- mm- dd- HH+:nn:ss 
 | dd-MM-yyyy HH:mm:ss 
 | yyyy mm dd 
 | yyyy- mm- dd- HH:nn:ss 
 | yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSS 
 | yyyy- mm- dd- 
 | mm- dd- yyyy HH:nn:ss 
 | yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm 
 | yyyy/mm/dd 
 | MMMddyyyy 
 | yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss 
 | dd- mm- yyyy 
 | MMMddyyyy HH:mm 
 | yyyyMMdd HH:mm:ss 
 | dd/mm/yyyy 
 | dd-MMM-yyyy HH:mm 
 | yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm 
 | dd.mm .yyyy 
 | yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss 
 | yyyy-dd-MM 
 | yyyy- dd- mm- 
 | yyyy-MM-dd 
 | yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS 
 | yyyy/dd/mm 
 | yyyyMMdd 
 | MM-dd-yyyy HH:mm:ss 
 | dd-/mm-/yyyy HH:nn 
 | dd-MM-yyyy 
 | MMM-dd-yyyy HH:mm:ss 
 | yyyy- mm- dd- HH:nn:ss 
 | dd-MMM-yyyy 
 | yyyyMMM 
 | yyyy/mm/dd HH:nn:ss 
 | ddMMMyyyy 
 | 
 | dd- mm- yyyy HH:nn:ss 
 | dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm

===== PAGE: date_validation_while_creating_export_jobs.html | Date Validation While Creating Export Jobs =====
## Date Validation While Creating Export Jobs
## Description
 While creating export jobs, MEDS Extractor includes date validation to ensure that you only enter valid dates. For example, when scheduling a job, you cannot leave the Start Date field blank. 
 In addition, export jobs with no end date now store the end date value as null data instead of "999-12-31T23:59:59Z". This change causes any applicable Job Run End Date fields in MEDS Extractor to be blank.
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 MEDS Extractor's date validation ensures that your export jobs contain viable parameters. This mitigates errors during the job creating process, saving time and effort in correcting malformed export job configurations.
 With the change to how MEDS Extractor stores export jobs' end dates, jobs with no end dates have a more consistent and intuitive appearance compared to the jobs' configuration. 
## User Impact
 While reviewing a recurring export job's Job Configuration page, when the job has no end date, then the Job Run End Date field appears blank.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None. 
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: derived_dataset_created_from_template_is_empty.html | Derived Dataset Created From Template Is Empty =====
## Derived Dataset Created From Template Is Empty
 When you publish a derived dataset project, you can choose to publish it as a template , which you can reuse for other derived dataset projects. Sometimes, however, when you use a template the new project's canvas shows no nodes or branches from the template.
## Symptoms
 When you create a Derived Dataset project using the From Template option, the created project's canvas appears as empty. 
## Causes
 Projects published before the August release are missing a parameter added after the August release that prevents relevant data to populate in a template. Editing and republishing affected projects adds that parameter, ensuring that the templates function as expected.
## Diagnostic
 N/A
## Resolution
- Open a derived project that the template is based on. 
- Change the final node of the project such that there is a net-zero change to the project's output, such as adding and then deleting a column. 
- Save the project. 
- From the Transformations > Projects page, identify the project, and select Publish to publish the project  without publishing it as a template. 
- Wait for the project to move from the Publishing status to the Published status. 
- Once has the Published status, select Publish as Template to update the template.

## Developer Center: Developer Center and Data Source Enhancements
 Important:

 Product 
 | Data Connect
 Version 
 | 2026.1.0
 Change Type 
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality)
 Functionality or Module 
 | Data Connect > Developer Center
 Summary of Changes 
 | 
 Important : The R SDK and Developer Center are only available as a limited release for selected users.
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The R02 release introduces stablity and usability improvements to the Developer Center, alongside significant updates to Veeva data handling and dataset metadata retrieval. 
 Developer Center Updates 
- Token Management Safety: The Developer Center now show a confirmation page when you attempt to disable or regenerate an authentication token. You must confirm the action to proceed, preventing accidental deactivation of access keys. 
 Get Datasets Function Enhancements 
 The datasets() function result now includes the following metadata attributes for each dataset:
- Dataset Name 
- Dataset Short Name 
- List of Collections (optional) 
- Dataset Type, for example: Derived Dataset, Rave, SFTP Import, Custom Code 
- Dataset Source Name 
- Activation Status (optional) 
- Dataset Status 
- Last Updated (optional) 
 In addition, when a Veeva form contains data collected across different CRF versions, the  Get Datasets and Dataset Version functions now list all CRF versions as a comma-separated list. 
 Benefit to Users 
 | 
- Security and Usability: The new confirmation pages in the Developer Center prevent the accidental disabling or regeneration of authentication tokens, reducing the risk of unintentional access disruptions 
- Standardization: Updating the API response to camelCase ensures consistency with standard REST API formats, simplifying integration for developers 
- Data Visibility: The Veeva update simplifies data management by consolidating multiple dataset versions into a single, comprehensive view. You can access a superset of data without navigating between disparate versions. 
- Improved Metadata : The enhanced datasets() output provides greater visibility into dataset properties, statuses, and origins, aiding in better data management and troubleshooting 
 Summary of User Impact 
 |

 All Data Connect users managing API keys in the Developer Center or interacting with Veeva data sources. 
 Impact to Workflow Processes 
- Developer Center: You must now confirm your action in a page when clicking Disable Token or Generate New Token. 
- Veeva EDC Data: You no longer need to manage multiple versions of a Veeva dataset. 
 Impact to Applications 
 No impact to other applications.
 Impact on Interface 
- Developer Center: A confirmation page appears when you select Disable Token or Generate New Token . 
 Impact to Existing Data or Settings 
 Existing Veeva studies migrate to the new versionless format. Previous versions are deprecated, and future imports utilize the superset schema. 
 User Impact Assessment

 Medium

===== PAGE: domain_and_concept_configuration.html | Domain and Concept Configuration =====
## Domain and Concept Configuration
## Description
 Platform Workbench expands your ability to configure domains and concepts on a data project in the ways outlined in the below table: 
 Workflow 
 Capability 
 | Domain and Concept Creation 
 | 
 You can:
- Add domains and concepts to a data project.
- If a data project is missing a domain, you can select the following domains in SDTM standards and related concepts to the project:
- Vital Signs (VS) 
- Labs (LB) 
- Concomitant or Prior Medications (CM) 
- Adverse Events (AE) 
- If a domain is missing a concept, you can add concepts to add to the domain. 
- You can remove unnecessary domains and concepts. 
 Note :  What are Concepts? 
 Concepts are biomedical semantic terms that have different industry-standard properties attached to them. Concepts help accelerate the data mapping and transformation process by automatically populating values in different data standards. Medidata includes several concepts, but you can also add your own concepts and save them to the library for the future.
 | Data Source Review 
 | You can:
- Review a domain’s data sources, see the source and target sources’ data and metadata, and: 
- Tag a target data source with details such as enrichment and source field-to-target mapping. 
- Map a source’s code list or category to specific controlled terminology or canonical syntax. 
- Preview the target data source’s data mapping. 
- Review a mapping’s data quality through alerts.
## Affected Roles
 Select internal users with permission to use Platform Workbench.
## Advantages
 With the expanded domain and concept configuration options, you can build projects that help reconcile the study design with data capture.
## User Impact
 None. This feature is available to select internal users.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None.
## Special Considerations
 This feature is not generally available and will be used for demo purposes by Medidata personnel.
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to RCM.

===== PAGE: download_data_extracts.html | Download Data Extracts =====
## Download Data Extracts
 After creating an export , you can download a zipped file containing the extracted data. The name of the zipped file and its contents follow the name and output configurations set while creating the export. You can download an export's data extracts from a given export's Run History screen.
 For details about specific data sets from extracted files, see  Available Exportable Data Objects .
 Tip : You can receive notifications about completed exports through email and the Medidata Message Center . See  View Exports Ready To Download Notifications for more information.
- Access Data Connect , and click   Exports   in the left-hand menu. 
- From the Exports screen, select an export. 
- Click the Export Run History tab. 
- Click >  Download, to download any available extracted data for the selected run. 
 Tip : You can download the extract for the latest run from the Exports screen by clicking an export's Options icon   >  Download Latest Run .
 Important :  The data extract file is only available through the interface for 60 days after a successful export run. However, you can send  a copy of the   extracted data to an external file location. Extract copies saved to external locations are   not subject to this download time limit. Client-side file location administrators specify the file retention periods and access permissions for these locations. The setup of, and access to, external repositories is not under Medidata control. For details on setting up external file locations, see  Configuring External File Connections .

===== PAGE: download_period_for_successful_exports_extended_to_60_days.html | Download Period for Successful Exports Extended to 60 Days =====
## Download Period for Successful Exports Extended to 60 Days
## Description
 You can download extracted files from an export job up to 60 days after the most recent successful export.
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 Previously, you had up to 24 hours after a successful export job to download the extracted files. While SFTP export has no limitation, the additional time gives you flexibility for when you can download your files, especially when an export job completes over a weekend or holiday.
## User Impact
 You can download extracted files up to 60 days after an export job successfully completes.

===== PAGE: edit_export.html | Edit Export =====
## Edit Export
 You can update export configurations. Updating an export creates another export version a fter updating an export. Future export executions reflect the most recent export version. You can update parameters such as: data type, scheduling frequency, and file type. 
- Access Data Connect , and click   Exports   in the left-hand menu to land on the Exports screen.
 Note : You can only see the exports you created unless you have Super User permission that allows you to see all exports in the study group. For details, see Data Connect Roles and Capabilities . 
- Identify an export to update, and click the Options icon >  Edit . 
- The edit export wizard appears with prefilled fields based on the current configuration. The edit wizard follows the same workflow as the export creation wizard on which you can update fields as necessary.
 Tip : You can also edit an export by clicking Edit from its Configuration screen.

===== PAGE: enable_recurring_imports_for_a_dataset.html | Enable Recurring Imports for a Dataset =====
## Enable Recurring Imports for a Dataset
 Imports fetches the active datasets from the data sources every hour. A dataset is inactive when ongoing import is disabled.
 Note : The data ingestion time depends on the quantity of the ingested file, and parallel data ingestions running simultaneously for a data source.
- Access Data Connect , and click   Imports   in the left-hand menu. 
- Click the more icon of the data source, and select View. 
- Click the dataset you want to activate. 
- Click Activate dataset on the upper right. 
 Tip :
- Inactive dataset imports have recurring status Recurring paused .  
- Active dataset imports have recurring status  Recurring. 
- To inactivate a dataset: 
- 
- Repeat steps 1 to 3. 
- Click Pause dataset on the upper right.

===== PAGE: enhanced_string_transformations_for_derived_variables.html | Transformations: Enhanced String Transformations for Derived Variables =====
## Transformations: Enhanced String Transformations for Derived Variables
 Important: 
- For a summary of changes done to these release notes, if any, see the revision history .. 
- For features and enhancements covered in previous releases, see the releases history . 
- For a summary of all features released, the released features index . 
## Change Details
 Product 
 | Data Connect
 Version 
 | 2026.1.0
 Change Type 
 | Enhancement (Updated Functionality)
 Functionality or Module 
 | Transformations > Derived Datasets
 Summary of Changes 
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 The R02 release introduces enhancements to string transformation functions used in derived datasets. 
- Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls): The Simple Concatenate transformation is now called Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls) . This function now automatically skips null values during concatenation, ensuring that the specified delimiter does not repeat when data points are missing. 
- Substring with Delimiter: The Substring transformation now includes the ability to define a specific delimiter, allowing you to extract substrings based on character patterns rather than just fixed positions. 
 Benefit to Users 
 | These updates provide greater flexibility and cleaner data output when manipulating string fields. 
- Improved Data Quality: By automatically skipping nulls during concatenation, the system prevents you from creating data strings with repeated delimiters, for example: converting "headache; fever;;;rash" to "headache; fever; rash". 
- Enhanced Extraction: The ability to substring by a delimiter allows for more dynamic text extraction, reducing the need for complex custom SQL or multiple transformation steps to isolate specific data segments. 
 Summary of User Impact 
 | 
 Impact to Users and Roles 
 All Data Connect users configuring derived datasets and transformations. 
 Impact to Workflow Processes 
- Concatenation: When using the concatenation function, you no longer need to configure logic to handle nulls manually. The Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls) function handles this automatically. 
- Substring: You can now input a specific character or string as a delimiter when configuring a Substring transformation 
 Impact to Applications 
 No impact to other applications.
 Impact on Interface 
- Transformation List: The Simple Concatenate option is now to Simple Concatenate (Ignore nulls) . 
- Configuration Panel: The Substring configuration panel now includes an input field for defining a delimiter. The previous "Ignore Nulls" parameter no longer exists in the concatenate configuration as it is now the default behavior. 
 Impact to Existing Data or Settings 
 You must review existing transformations to ensure the new "Ignore Nulls" behavior aligns with your expected data outputs. 
 User Impact Assessment 
 | Medium
 Learning Resource Updates 
 | There are no additional relevant resources.
 For more articles and videos, see  Generate Derived Datasets and the Data Connect Video Library . 
 Special Considerations 
 | To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal. 
## Definitions
 Acronym Glossary 
 For definitions of the different acronyms that can appear in the Knowledge Hub, see  Global Acronym Glossary .

- Low – There is no change to current software behavior.
- Moderate – Change includes corrections or improvements that may change user interaction with the software, such as minor UI or minor functionality changes.
- High – Change includes software changes which require review of existing settings, workflow and/or operational procedures to ensure correct user interaction with the software.

## Export: Updated Create and Edit Export Workflows
## What Is New?
 Technical changes to Data Connect's Create Export and Edit Export workflows to: 
- Improve performance and backend processes.  
- For the users that are redirected from Data Connect's Transformation to Create Export workflow can see pre-populated fields. 
- You can select multiple studies in a single export. 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 No Impact to the workflow process. However, you can select multiple studies while creating the export or when you edit an export.
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications.
## How Do I Benefit?
 The transition from Data Connect's Transformations module to the Create Export workflow facilitates a smooth  transition that includes some pre-populated fields. This reduces manual input and minimizes the number of clicks required to create an export. By automating data population, you can focus on configuring the export with minimal effort. 
 The multiselect feature in the study drop down enhances the user experience.

 No changes to the user interface.

===== PAGE: export_configuration_compatibility_by_data_type.html | Export Configuration Compatibility by Data Type =====
## Export Configuration Compatibility by Data Type
 You can create exports to extract different types of data from the Medidata platform. Some configuration options differ between data types. The below tables highlight the job configuration options for each data type.
 For details on the different data types, see  Available Exportable Data Objects .
## Data Export Type Compatibility
 You can set exports to extract data cumulatively or incrementally:
- Cumulative – Each export instance exports all available data. 
- Incremental – Each export instance only exports data changes since the previous run. The first incremental run is cumulative. 
 For details about configuring an export's data export type, see Set Data Export Type .

## Repeating Export Compatibility
 You can set exports to run immediately, schedule export to run at a future date, or schedule exports to repeat at a regular frequency. Repeating options include:
- Minutely – The export runs each minute, starting from the start date and time. 
- Hourly  – The export runs each hour, starting from the start date and time. 
- Daily – The export runs at the selected hour each day, starting from the start date. 
- Weekly  – The export runs on the selected hour on the selected days of every week.   The export first executes on the next selected day and hour on or after the scheduled start date. 
- Monthly  –   The export runs at the selected date and hour each month. Exports only run on the months containing that date. 
- Last day of month  –   The export runs at the selected hour on the last day of each month. This option ensures that exports run on the last day of every month, irrespective of the number of days in the month. 
 For details about scheduling jobs, see  Schedule Export .

===== PAGE: export_dataset_snapshots_from_project.html | Export Dataset Snapshots from Project =====
## Export Dataset Snapshots from Project
 You can export the data related to the data project’s transformations as a snapshot. 
- Access Data Connect , and click   Transformations   in the left-hand menu. 
- Expand a published project. 
- Click More , and select one of the following:
- Set Up Cloud Extraction – Links to the Export capability, where you can set up export jobs to download data. For details, see Extracting Data with Exports . 
- Export CSV Locally – Downloads the datasets in the project as a CSV file.
 Important : The exported file has a 500 record limit. To export more records, use the Set Up Cloud Extraction option.

===== PAGE: export_job_editing_now_available.html | Export Job Editing Now Available =====
## Export Job Editing Now Available
## Description
 You can now update jobs. Extractor creates another job version a fter updating a job. Future job executions reflect the most recent job version. You can update the following parameters: 
- Study – You can update the study and add studies to the job. 
- Data Type – You can update the data type and add data types to the job. 
- Export Data Sets – You can update the selection of data sets to include in or exclude from the extract. 
- Scheduling Frequency – You can update the job run frequency. 
- Data Export Type – You can change job from cumulative to Incremental and vice versa. 
- File Type – You can update and select between multiple file types depending on the job, such as comma-separated value (CSV) or SAS (XPT) for EDC jobs. 
- SFTP – You can update which external SFTP location the job uses. 
 Important : You cannot update a job's name.
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 You can update your jobs directly and forgo needing to create more jobs and archiving older ones to address configuration changes. 
## User Impact
 On the Export Jobs screen, each job's options menu includes the Edit Job button. You can also find the Edit Job button on a specific job's Job Configuration screen. Clicking Edit Job takes you to the export job edit wizard with prefilled fields based on the current configuration. The edit wizard follows the same workflow as the job creation wizard on which you can update fields as necessary. Editing a job creates a job version. Extractor uses the most recent job version when running jobs. 
 Various parts of Extractor's Job Details screen include updates to support this feature:
- The updated Job Configuration tab: 
- Includes a version menu that you can use to review the job's version history. 
- Adds the following fields: 
- Created Date – The job version's creation date. The field shows "-" for new jobs. 
- Modified Date – The job version's modification date. This field reflects when the job changed from version x to version x+1 and shows "-" for new jobs. 
- Version – The job's version number. This field shows "1" for new jobs. 
- The updated Job Run History tab: 
- Includes the Version column. Clicking the version takes you to that version's configuration details.  
- Includes the View Version Details button on a specific job run's menu. Clicking View Version Details also takes you to that version's configuration details. 
- The added Versions tab includes a list of the job's versions. You can click a version's View Version Details button to see that version's configuration details. 
## Knowledge Updates
 Here are links to new and updated content to support these updates: 
- Review Export Configuration  (updated) 
- Review Export Run History (updated) 
- Edit Export (new) 
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: export_stages.html | Export Stages =====
## Export Stages
 You can create exports to extract data from the Medidata platform. The flowchart below shows the overall data extraction lifecycle.
 EXTRACTOR_FlowChart_Master_JobStages_ENG
- You use Data Connect to create an export, which results in one of the following:
- Data Connect creates the export. 
- Data Connect fails to create the export. Review the export's settings and try creating another job. 
- After creating the export, Data Connect runs the export at the specified time period. 
- Data Connect processes the export, 
- If the export is successful, then all the export's select datasets are available for downloading.
- The system adds a link to download the data extract from the user interface. 
- If the export has an SFTP configuration , then the system sends the data extract to the SFTP location. 
- If the export fails, then the export process fails. You can review the job's status and details in the user interface. The job status includes a Trace ID to help identify and troubleshoot the root cause with Medidata Support.

===== PAGE: exports.html | Exports =====
## Exports
 Exports is a Data Connect capability that exports various types of data, such as clinical and CTMS data, from the Medidata platform in various file types. 
## What Is Exports?
 Exports is data export tool that packages various types of data, such as clinical and CTMS data, from the Medidata platform in various file types for downloading. You can specify multiple datasets from specific studies and environments. Exports deliver the exported datasets as individual files compressed into standard .zip files. 
## What Is the Purpose of Exports?
 Exports allows you to download various data from the Medidata platform, including transformed data outputted from data projects made in Transformations . You can run exports on a recurring schedule, which allows you to track changes made to different data sources to ensure the data is clean and within expectations. 
## Who Uses Exports?
 Data managers, clinical data scientists, biostatisticians, data scientists, and programmers.
## How Does Exports Work?
 You can create and run exports by specifying the origin and type of data to export from the Medidata platform . 
 To create an export , you must use the Create Export workflow to configure the exports parameters, such as when and how often the export runs and what type of data it delivers for each export. After you create the exports and the export successfully runs, you can then download the extracted datasets. You can either download files directly from the interface, or configure external locations to send files to instead. 
 Important :  Unsupported Data 
 Data Connect does not support variables such as coding dictionaries, coded terms, and code lists. While you can import datasets that includes these data, you cannot see or use them in Data Connect. However, these data can appear as-is in RBQM and Data Surveillance when you activate the associated dataset. For details, see Unsupported Variables in Data Connect . 
## What Are the Benefits of Exports?
 Exports provide:
- A central location to download data from the Medidata platform for auditing and view purposes. 
- Secure methods to download your data locally or to an external location. 
## How Do I Access Exports?
 Access Data Connect , and click   Exports   in the left-hand menu.

===== PAGE: exports_-_export_datasets_with_data_connect.html | Exports: Export Datasets with Data Connect =====
## Exports: Export Datasets with Data Connect
## What Is New?
 Users can now access Extractor's data export capabilities through Data Connect's Export module. Data Connect inherits every Extractor capability, and includes the Transformed Data export type that exports datasets created through the Transformations module. 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
- All users with access to Data Connect 
- All Extractor users 
 Important : Existing Extractor users must now access their export jobs through Data Connect. This change does not affect any existing job configurations, but only changes how you access your jobs.
## Workflow Process
 There is no impact on the workflow process. The existing workflow to create and manage export jobs is unchanged. 
## Other Applications
 Extractor and its data export capabilities is no longer accessible directly through the Medidata landing page. Users must access its capabilities through Data Connect.
## How Do I Benefit?
 The integration with Data Connect enables a seamless data management and. export experience for Data Connect users.
## Where Do I See the Change?
 Data Connect includes the Exports tab where users can access its data export capabilities. Extractor is no longer accessible and is instead replaced by Exports. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 There are several knowledge articles that cover how to use Data Connect's Exports module, including: 
- Exports 
- User Data Listings - Export Types and Methods 
- Rave Data Extract File Type Differences 
- Export Configuration Compatibility by Data Type 
- Export Stages 
- Available Exportable Data Objects 
- Configuring External File Connections 
- Extracting Data with Exports 
 Important : Documentation pertaining to Extractor is no longer accessible due to redundancy with Data Connect's Exports documentation, Any links to Extractor documentation will redirect to the Data Connect documentation. Update your links accordingly.
## What Else Should I Know?
 Extractor is no longer accessible as a standalone capability. All users must use Data Connect's Exports module to create and manage export jobs.

## Exports Releases History
 The following lists all releases for Data Connect's Exports capability before the June 2024 release, and the features or enhancements introduced in each.
 Important : As of the 2024.2.0 release, Extractor is a part of the Data Connect solution as its Exports capability. It is now "Data Connect - Exports" or "Exports". For details what the 2024.2.0 release entails, and following releases, see   Data Connect Releases History , 
 Code Continuity Across Releases
 All Extractor releases are cumulative. That is, each release or software update builds on the prior releases as noted in the release notes. For example,  Software Update 2023.2.1 includes all the release 2023.2.0  code, which in turn is based on the  release  2023.1.0 code, and so on. Any updates between releases or s oftware updates (urgent or otherwise) appear in the release notes.

===== PAGE: externalized_api_endpoints_for_meds_extractor.html | Externalized API Endpoints for MEDS Extractor =====
## Externalized API Endpoints for MEDS Extractor
## Description
 You can use Medidata's Clinical Cloud API endpoints to download data from export jobs using MEDS Extractor resources. The Medidata platform identifies your export jobs, job executions, and sends file download links through the endpoints. You can use the following endpoints:
- Get File Download URL – Returns an AWS URL where you can download MEDS Extractor files based on the Job Execution UUID, in ZIP format. 
- Get All Export Jobs – Returns a list of jobs for the current API user and the specified CDS UUID. 
- Get Job Details – Returns job details for the specified Job UUID and CDS UUID. 
- Get Latest Execution Details – Returns the latest job execution details. 
- Get All Job Executions – Returns a list of job executions for the specified Job UUID. 
- Get File Location Details – Returns the specified file location's details. 
- Get All File Locations – Returns a list of file location details for the specified CDS UUID. 
- Get Job Execution – Returns the details of the specified job execution. 
## Affected Roles
 All roles with permission to use Clinical Cloud RESTful API Resources. For details, see Get Started with Medidata Platform RESTful API Resources .
## Advantages
 You can programmatically download files using Medidata's API, giving you more options to download data from the Medidata platform.
## User Impact
 The addition of externalized MEDS Extractor resources does not affect your use of the MEDS Extractor user interface.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None. You can find details on the Clinical Cloud API Changelog . 
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: extract_user_login_history_data.html | Extract User Login History Data =====
## Extract User Login History Data
## Description
 You can now extract data on users’ login history. By creating an Audit Master Data export job at the client division level, you can extract the user login history data pertaining to all the users within the client division.
## Affected Roles
 All roles with permission to extract Audit Master Data. For details, see MEDS Extractor Roles and Permissions.
## Advantages
 You can extract and download users' login history from every study of your client division without needing to select each study. 
## User Impact
 You can only set export jobs to extract from client division level during the job creation process. You cannot edit existing export jobs to extract from the client division level. This feature does not affect existing export jobs.
 During the New Export Job workflow's Select Source step, after selecting the Client Division Level option, you can select Audit Master Data.
 When exporting Client Division Level Audit Master Data, the extracted files contains the User Login History data object. 
## Knowledge Space Updates
 The MEDS Extractor Knowledge Space provides new and updated content to support this feature/enhancement: 
- New
- Client Division Level Audit Master Data - Quick Start 
- Client Division Level Audit Master Data Objects 
- Updated
- Create Export Job at the Client Division Level 
- Select Data for Client Division Level Jobs 
- Schedule Client Division Level Job 
- Set Export Type for Client Division Level Job 
- Review Export Job Configuration 
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: extracting_data_with_exports.html | Extracting Data with Exports =====
## Extracting Data with Exports
 You create exports to enable the extraction of datasets from the Medidata platform  for any studies and environments you have access to . Depending on your role, you can perform one or more of the following actions:
- Create Export 
- Download Data Extracts 
- Review Exports 
- Rerun Export 
- Archive Export 
- Edit Export 
## Export Considerations
- Your platform role must include both the Unblinded User and Data Extracts building blocks to access the Exports tab and create exports. For details about granting access see  Grant Access to Data Connect . 
- If your role has a view restriction for at least one data point of a selected dataset, then you cannot extract the dataset. 
- All data types extract as individual datasets in  c omma-separated value (.csv) files. You can also choose to extract Rave data in the SAS (.xpt) file format. The export process compresses these files as a single .zip file. You can download the .zip file after a successful export run. 
- When you configure an export to extract Rave data in the SAS file format, the resulting .zip file contains both .csv files and .xpt files. 
- All audit exports, including those related to Clinical Data Studio and Data Connect, can take up to 24 hours to run. This time depends on the configured date range and the number of audits for the study. If the audit export fails, consider adjusting the date range. 
## Data Extraction with APIs
 You can also programmatically extract data from the Medidata platform by using Clinical Cloud API resources. For details, see the Download Data File Extract from the Medidata Platform workflow and the Data Import and Export resource type on the Medidata Platform API publication in the Knowledge Hub.

===== PAGE: extractor_now_compatiable_with_platform_workbench.html | Extractor Now Compatiable with Platform Workbench =====
## Extractor Now Compatiable with Platform Workbench
## What Is New?
 Extractor now includes the Transformed Data export type that exports data set projects from Platform Workbench. Moreover, you can use Extractor functionality from Platform Workbench interface, including job administration, scheduling, and delivery configuration. 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All users with access to Platform Workbench. 
## Workflow Process
 There is no impact on the workflow process. The existing workflow to create and manage export jobs is unchanged. 
## Other Applications
 Platform Workbench now includes the Exports tab. Clicking Exports opens Extractor's interface where you can create Transformed Data export jobs and perform other actions with Extractor. 
## How Do I Benefit?
 You can export published data pertaining to data sets and transformations you create in Platform Workbench in the same way as you export other data from Extractor. Moreover, the integration with Platform Workbench enables an integrated export experience for Platform Workbench users. 
## Where Do I See the Change?
 There is no change on the Extractor interface. However, Platform Workbench now includes the Exports tab where you can create export jobs, including the new Transformed Data export job. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 No additional information is available for this update.
## What Else Should I Know?
 You can still use the Extractor interface to create export jobs. 
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .
