===== PAGE: transformations__improved_date_format_processing_in_field_transformation.html | Transformations: Improved Date Format Processing in Field Transformation =====
## Transformations: Improved Date Format Processing in Field Transformation
## What Is New?
 When field transformations convert date strings into the Date format, acceptable strings are no longer case sensitive. Previously transformations could only convert strings in the case-sensitive ddMMMyyyy format such as "10Jan2024", but now it can accept other raw value strings in the same format such as "10JAN2024" or "10jan2024". 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 No impact to the workflow process.
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications.
## How Do I Benefit?
 Users no longer need to modify raw date strings in the ddMMMyyyy format before using them in Data Connect. 
## Where Do I See the Change?
 There is no change to the user experience. This does affect how Data Connect processes date strings for datasets. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 There are no additional relevant resources. 
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: transformations__project_archive_ability_now_available.html | Transformations: Project Archive Ability Now Available =====
## Transformations: Project Archive Ability Now Available
## What Is New?
 You can now archive projects from the Transformations > Projects page. There are two new building blocks that control who can archive projects:  
- Manage Transformation Projects – You can archive your own projects. Medidata recommends this setting for most users. 
- Transformation Admin – You can archive any user's project in the study group. 
 The Projects page also includes a new Usage Limits button. Clicking it shows you the count of published and activated projects you have across all environments, and how many remaining of your quota you can publish or activate.
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
- Project owners and creators 
- Administrators 
## Workflow Process
 Archiving projects removes them from the list. If you archive published or activated projects, then you must republish or reactivate them if you unarchive them later.
## Other Applications
- Data Surveillance and RBQM – If you archive a project with activated datasets, then any data already delivered downstream to Data Surveillance and RBQM remains there as stale data. Downstream users do not receive notifications if an activated dataset has been archived in Data Connect. 
- Data Connect >  Exports – Archival and unarchival actions appear in Data Connect data source exports. 
## How Do I Benefit?
 The ability to archive projects addresses user feedback requesting the ability to delete erroneous projects. Archiving provides an alternative to permanent deletion, which maintains compliance while helping you manage cluttered project lists by hiding irrelevant items.
## Where Do I See the Change?
 The Archive button is available on any given projects from the Transformations > Projects page. You can use the filters on the Projects page to show or hide archived projects. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Use the following links to find new and updated content in support of this feature:
- Data Connect Roles and Capabilities (updated) 
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: transformations__project_capacity_limits_for_derived_datasets.html | Transformations: Project Capacity Limits for Derived Datasets =====
## Transformations: Project Capacity Limits for Derived Datasets
 Important: 
- For a summary of changes done to these release notes, if any, see the revision history .. 
- For features and enhancements covered in previous releases, see the releases history . 
- For a summary of all features released, the released features index . 
## Change Details
 Product 
 | Data Connect
 Version 
 | 2026.1.0
 Change Type 
 | Feature (New Functionality)
 Functionality or Module 
 | Transformations > Derived Datasets
 Summary of Changes 
 | 
 As highlighted in the Release Newsletter : 
 Data Connect now includes a Project Capacity progress bar within Derived Datasets projects. This visual indicator shows the total execution time consumed by the project's operations against a hard limit of 60 seconds. 
 The system calculates capacity usage as you build a derived dataset on the canvas . The progress bar provides visual feedback using the following color indicators: 
- Green : 0% to 74% capacity 
- Yellow : 75% to 90% capacity 
- Red/Orange : 91% to 100% capacity 
 Benefit to Users 
 | This enhancement provides critical visibility into a project's complexity and execution time. By monitoring the Project Capacity bar, you can ensure your configurations remain within system limits, allowing you to optimize operations and successfully publish projects without encountering processing issues. 
 Summary of User Impact 
 | 
 Impact to Users and Roles 
 All Data Connect users configuring derived datasets 
 Impact to Workflow Processes 
- Monitoring Capacity : You can now monitor the Project Capacity meter in the top header bar to track usage . 
- Validation Blocking : If a project exceeds 100% capacity, the system blocks you from adding more nodes to the canvas. You must decrease the project scope, such as by reducing nodes or modifying operators, to unlock the canvas . 
- Publishing: While the Publish button remains enabled, attempting to publish a project that is over capacity leads to a validation error. 
 Impact to Applications 
 No impact to other applications.
 Impact on Interface 
- Progress Bar: A labeled Project Capacity linear progress bar appears in a persistent location on the header . 
- Info Icon: An info icon next to the bar shows a tooltip explaining that reaching maximum capacity prevents publishing . 
- Error Messages : Specific error messages appear when you reach capacity, such as: "This configuration exceeds the project's capacity limit and it cannot be published" . 
 Impact to Existing Data or Settings 
 The system exempts existing projects that exceed the capacity limit as "legacy exceptions." It does not automatically unpublish these projects, but does open them in a Project Lock state.  In this state, you cannot add nodes, but can delete node or remove existing operators. 
 User Impact Assessment 
 | High 
 Learning Resource Updates 
 | 
 The following lists any new or added resources that support this change:
- New Article:  Project Capacity 
 For more articles and videos, see  Transformations and the Data Connect Video Library . 
 Special Considerations 
 | To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal.

===== PAGE: transformations_and_developer_center__data_connect_r_sdk_integration.html | Transformations and Developer Center: Data Connect R SDK Integration =====
## Transformations and Developer Center: Data Connect R SDK Integration
 Important : This feature is available as a limited release for selected users.
## What Is New?
 The Data Connect R SDK (Software Development Kit) is now available as a limited release to enable integration with R. With this capability, statisticians and programmers can access and manipulate datasets through  RStudio to publish back into Data Connect. 
 To support this, Transformations includes the new Custom Code project type to host datasets published from R. In addition, Data Connect now includes Developer Center, from which you can generate authentication tokens.  
 The new Manage Custom Code Projects building block grants access to Developer Center and the ability to create Custom Code projects in Transformations.
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 Statisticians and Programmers who use R.
## Workflow Process
 No impact to the workflow process. This is a new capability.  However, if you intend to publish datasets transformed in RStudio into Data Connect, you must create a Custom Code project in Transformations to host the change and generate a project token.   
## Other Applications
 RBQM and Data Surveillance has the following restrictions for custom code datasets:
- You cannot submit queries on datasets transformed by custom code. 
- You cannot use these datasets for derived variables, stake datasets, and event analysis mapping. However, you can use these datasets for listings, KRIs, QTLs, critical data, and self-service visualization. 
## How Do I Benefit?
 The R SDK enables R integration with Data Connect, saving you time and effort integrating with new data sources. With this, you can use your existing R infrastructure and tools for advanced manipulation.
## Where Do I See the Change?
- Data Connect > Transformations – You can create the Custom Code project type. 
- Data Connect – You can select the Developer Center tab. 
- Data Surveillance – You can find custom code datasets in the Custom Code category. 
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Use the following links to find new and updated content in support of this feature:
- Developer Center (new) 
- Generate Custom Code Projects 
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: troubleshoot_common_errors_for_failed_exports.html | Troubleshoot Common Errors for Failed Exports =====
## Troubleshoot Common Errors for Failed Exports
 If an export fails, you can view the cause on the Error column of the Export execution details screen. 
 Refer to the table below for diagnosing and correcting the most common issues. After you adjust the configurations, you can rerun the export by clicking Run Now.
 Note : If you are unable to correct the issue by yourself, open a helpdesk ticket.
 Error Message  
 Troubleshoot 
 | Data extraction failed 
 | 
 For Data Connect Exports :
- Ensure that you can preview the datasets in the Data Connect's Datasets. 
- Ensure that you are assigned to a role with Extract Transformed Data building block. 
 For Rave (Raw) :
- Ensure that you are assigned to a role with Extract Transformed Data and Unblinded Data building blocks. 
- Ensure that you have permissions for all sites and countries for the study environment. 
- Verify that the study is onboarded to MEDS with your Medidata representative. 
 For CTMS and Master Data :
- Ensure that you are assigned to correct building blocks including Unblinded Data building block. 
 Note : See  Data Connect Roles and Capabilities for more information. 
 | JSON processing failed 
 | 
- Ensure all the field names do not contain special characters. 
 Note : See  Rave Data Extract File Type Differences for more information. 
 | SAS processing failed 
 | 
- Ensure your SAS field names do not contain special characters. 
 Note : See  Rave Data Extract File Type Differences for more information. 
 | SFTP delivery failed 
 | 
- Ensure that your SFTP server credentials have not changed or expired. 
- Ensure that the server is accessible outside of your private network or VPN connection. 
- Ensure that the account has full read or write access to the server. 
 Note : See  Configuring Exports to Deliver to SFTP for more information. 
 | FTPS delivery failed 
 | 
- Ensure that your FTPS server credentials have not changed or expired. 
- Ensure that the server is accessible outside of your private network or VPN connection. 
- Ensure that the account has full read or write access to the server. 
- If you use Medidata hosted FTPS server, ensure that you are using Implicit encryption and port 990. 
 Note : See  Configuring Exports to Deliver to FTPS Connections for more information.
 | S3 delivery failed 
 | 
- Ensure that your S3 bucket is registered with Medidata. If not, open a helpdesk ticket to register. 
- Ensure the S3 bucket policy in your AWS console matches the Medidata policy. 
- Ensure the values entered in the interface to match with your AWS console as the S3 bucket name and path are case-sensitive. 
 Note : See  Configuring Exports to Deliver to Amazon S3 Connections for more information.
 | Export failed due to an internal error. 
 | 
- Open a helpdesk ticket.

===== PAGE: unit_conversion_now_available.html | Unit Conversion Now Available =====
## Unit Conversion Now Available
## What Is New?
 You can configure unit conversations to transform data set fields from one unit into another unit for later mapping. Platform Workbench uses a Quantities, Units, Dimensions, and Types (QUDT) industry library to automatically identify relevant source and target units and analytes. The QUDT supports over 1500 units resulting in over 1.5 million pairwise combinations. With unit conversion, you can automatically generate and apply conversion factors, and custom configurations. 
 After adding one or more data sets, you can configure unit conversions on each form by defining unit fields for their respective unit forms. This entails: 
- Selecting a value for the source form. 
- Selecting a unit for the target form. 
- Selecting an analyte as necessary. 
 Platform Workbench performs quality checking for your selections, and groups related source units. From there, you select a target unit for the group or select units for individual source units. Platform Workbench uses an internal library to automatically identify relevant source and target units, and you can manually identify units as necessary.
 Transformation modules for other projects also now include the datetime difference module.
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 There is no change to the workflow process. This feature extends existing project creation capabilities.  
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications.
## How Do I Benefit?
 You can standardize the units within your data models, especially when combining data from multiple sources.
## Where Do I See the Change?
 When you create a data project in the Transformations, you can select the Unit Conversion project type.
## Where Do I Learn More?
 Supplementary help articles will be available at a future date.
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: unsupported_variables_in_data_connect.html | Unsupported Variables in Data Connect =====
## Unsupported Variables in Data Connect
 Data Connect does not support specific variables found in source data systems. However, some of these variables can still appear in Data Surveillance or Risk-Based Quality Management (RBQM) when you activate a derived dataset from Data Connect . Review the following exclusions to understand current data availability limitations. 
## Excluded Variables 
 Variable 
 Description 
 Example 
 | Coder-related Variables 
 | Coding levels, supplemental terms, and component terms.
 The following fields appear in Rave and transfer into Data Surveillance – but not RBQM – once you activate the dataset:
- CMTRT_ATC1 
- CMTRT_ATC2 
- CMTRT_ATC3 
- CMTRT_ATC4 
- CMTRT_PT 
- CMTRT_PRODUCT 
- CMTRT_PRODUCTSYNONYM 
 | The Coder term field variable CMTRT from the CM form
 | Decoded Codelists 
 | When a field in Rave is a Dictionary value, the field has 2 associated result fields: the string value and the coded value. Data Connect only shows the coded value, but Data Surveillance also adds the string value suffixed with "_TXT".  
 | The CMYN field variable includes:
- 'Y' as the coded value, with "Yes" as the string value 
- 'N' as the coded value, with "No" as the string value 
 Data Connect only shows CMYN and its coded value, while Data Surveillance also includes CMYN_TXT and the associated string value.

- Update the data source's connection details.

===== PAGE: updated_sftp_support.html | Updated SFTP Support =====
## Updated SFTP Support
## Description
 The Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) location management workflow includes the following updates:
- You can test SFTP connections in MEDS Extractor. 
- You can specify if an SFTP location is available for data export, data import, or both. Data export is only available with MEDS Extractor, and data import is only available with MEDS Ingestor. 
## Affected Roles
 All roles with the Extractor Admin building block.
## Advantages
 With the updated SFTP support, you have more robust control over the functionality of your SFTP locations. With the added testing functionality, you can ensure a location is functional before adding it to an export job.
## User Impact
 From the Output Locations screen, you can: 
- Test SFTP locations by identifying a location and clicking the Options icon >  Test . 
- Review the Data Direction column to identify locations' data transfer direction. 
- When adding or updating an SFTP location, you can select the location's Data Transfer Direction: 
- Export Data  –  Readies the location to receive data. 
- Import Data   – Readies the location to send  data. 
 This enhancement does not change any settings for currently functional SFTP locations. 
## Special Considerations
 None.

===== PAGE: updates_to_export_job_list_screen.html | Updates to Export Job List Screen =====
## Updates to Export Job List Screen
## Description
 MEDS Extractor's Export Jobs screen includes the following changes:
- When you sort the job list by the Last Run parameter, the list now sorts by 'In Progress', then by the dates in reverse chronological order. 
- The Frequency column includes the job's activation status with its schedule frequency. With this added subtext, you can see if a job has a future run instance. 
- When filtering jobs, the 'Schedule' filtering parameter is now 'Job Activity'. When filtering by Job Activity, you can filter by Active or Inactive jobs. 
- When you manually run a job by clicking Run Now, the job list page automatically refreshes.  
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 The Export Jobs screen updates provide a more streamlined and intuitive experience when viewing, sorting, and filtering export jobs.
## User Impact
 See Description.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 The MEDS Extractor Knowledge Space provides new and updated content to support this enhancement: 
- Review Exports List (updated) 
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: upgrade_datasets_to_latest_version.html | Upgrade Datasets to Latest Version =====
## Upgrade Datasets to Latest Version
 When you use Rave data or imported external data in your projects, the dataset versions can change when a user changes form components and other metadata. When this occurs, you must upgrade the dataset within Data Connect to ensure proper data flow with the latest dataset configurations.
 Important : This capability is unavailable for Veeva EDC datasets.
- Access Data Connect , and click  Transformations   in the left-hand menu. 
- Open a derived dataset project. 
- On the Dataset Lineage tab > dataset canvas, identify a node with the New Version Available icon . 
- Click More  >  Map to latest version . 
- Do one of the following:
- Manually map the dataset. 
- Click More > Run auto-mapping .
 Important : When mapping, Medidata recommends that the source variable and target field must be of the same datatype. However, if the datatypes do not match, you can perform a datatype conversion using Transformation Instructions.
- Click Validate to preview results and perform quality checking. 
- Click  Apply . 
- Repeat steps 3-7 as necessary.

===== PAGE: upgrade_derived_datasets_to_enable_rave_query.html | Upgrade Derived Datasets to Enable Rave Query =====
## Upgrade Derived Datasets to Enable Rave Query
 When you activate a derived dataset using Rave data, the connection allows you to directly open Rave queries in Data Surveillance. This functionality is available by default for newly created derived datasets. However, existing derived datasets require manual upgrades to enable this functionality.
## Considerations and Limitations
- Derived datasets created before May 23, 2025 must be upgraded and then reactivated to enable direct Rave querying in Data Surveillance. 
- Derived datasets created on or after May 23, 2025 do not need to be upgraded. 
- Derived datasets using the following operations from Rave datasets will be enabled for direct Rave querying in Data Surveillance:
- Join 
- Union 
- Replicate or Filter 
- Medidata recommends upgrading no more than five datasets at a time per study. To upgrade more datasets, wait for any queued upgrades to finish. 
## Upgrade Dataset
- Access Data Connect , and click  Datasets   in the left-hand menu. 
 The Datasets landing page shows a banner that says "Upgrade to enable queries for derived datasets in Data Surveillance". 
- Click View Available Upgrades on the banner. 
- Locate a dataset from the right-hand menu and click Upgrade . 
- After a successful upgrade, activate the dataset for Data Surveillance.

===== PAGE: view_and_manage_templates.html | View and Manage Templates =====
## View and Manage Templates
 Data Connect includes the ability to create standardized, reusable, and version-controlled templates that capture and apply the transformation logic from source projects. This ensures uniformity and acceleration in the setup process across diverse transformation needs. 
 Important : You need the following building blocks and corresponding permissions: 
- View Library Templates : To view and use templates. 
- Manage Library Templates : To create and edit templates 
 When you add these building blocks, ensure that you add them at the client level and not at the study level. 
## View Templates
- Access Data Connect . 
- Click the Platform Library   tab. 
- Using the Search field, enter the template name, 
- Click the More icon > View template . 
- Click a data source or the node to view template details. 
- Select a version from the menu to view the different versions of the template.
 Important : You can only use the latest version of the template when you create a project with the template.
- As necessary, click  Modify the source project to open the source project and update it.
## Create a Project from Template
 You can create a derived dataset transformation project with templates. 
- Access Data Connect , and click  Transformations  in the left-hand menu. 
- Click   Add Project , and enter the following settings:
- Project Name   – Enter a project name. 
- Project Type   – Select   Derived datasets . 
- Select Setup From Template . 
- Select a template from the library. 
- Click   Add project . 
The system automatically pre-populates the new derived dataset canvas with the template's predefined configurations including dataset schema and mapping logic. 
- As necessary,  change the configuration .  
- As necessary, publish the project . 
 Important : Only Librarians or Admins can add or publish templates.

===== PAGE: view_exports_failed_notifications.html | View Exports Failed Notifications =====
## View Exports Failed Notifications
 Through the Medidata system, you receive the following automated notifications: 
- Information   – Informs you when the export fails in Data Connect - Exports.
 Notifications are processed and sent through the Medidata's  Notification Center . 
 You can view exports failed notifications in two forms: 
- Email notifications with a deep-link sent to your email inbox. 
- In-app notifications are accessible by way of the Medidata platform user interface. 
 Exports failed notifications keep you closely informed regarding failed exports and logged errors. 
 Note : To receive or discontinue exports failed notifications, contact your Medidata Professional Services representative.
## View Exports Failed Notifications Through Email 
- Open the email notification you receive from Medidata. 
- Click  View in Notification Center .
 The Notifications page shows a message regarding the data refresh. 
## View Exports Failed Notifications in the Notification Center
- Log in to   iMedidata.com . 
- Click the   Notifications   icon     in the iMedidata toolbar.
 Note : An icon with a red circle    indicates new, unread notifications. 
The Notifications page shows all notifications you have received, listed in order from newest at top.
 Note : You can continuously scroll through notifications to the end. When you reach the bottom, if there are more notifications, then the page automatically shows the notifications. 
- Optional . In the left-hand pane, click to filter the Notifications page to show specific notifications by type: All, Information, Warning, or Critical.

===== PAGE: view_exports_ready_to_download_notifications.html | View Exports Ready To Download Notifications =====
## View Exports Ready To Download Notifications
 Through the Medidata system, you receive the following automated notification: 
- Information   – Informs you when the export is successful and ready to download in Data Connect - Exports.
 Notifications are processed and sent through the Medidata's  Notification Center . 
 You can view Ready to Download notifications in two forms: 
- Email notifications with a deep-link sent to your email inbox. 
- In-app notifications are accessible by way of the Medidata platform user interface. 
 Exports ready to download notifications keep you closely informed regarding successful exports. 
 Note : To receive or discontinue exports ready to download notifications, contact your Medidata Professional Services representative.
## View Exports Ready to Download Notifications Through Email 
- Open the email notification you receive from Medidata. 
- Click  View in Notification Center .
 The Notifications page shows a message regarding the data refresh. 
## View Exports Ready to Download Notifications in the Notification Center
- Log in to   iMedidata.com . 
- Click the   Notifications   icon     in the iMedidata toolbar.
 Note : An icon with a red circle    indicates new, unread notifications. 
The Notifications page shows all notifications you have received, listed in order from newest at top.
 Note : You can continuously scroll through notifications to the end. When you reach the bottom, if there are more notifications, then the page automatically shows the notifications. 
- Optional . In the left-hand pane, click to filter the Notifications page to show specific notifications by type: All, Information, Warning, or Critical.

===== PAGE: workflow_for_new_export_job.html | Workflow for New Export Job =====
## Workflow for New Export Job
## Description
 MEDS Extractor now uses a multi-step workflow when you create export jobs.   MEDS Extractor validates each step before you can move to the next , simplifying error correction.
 With the updated export job workflow, you can:
- Select which data to extract. 
- Name export jobs. 
- Name extracted files. 
## Affected Roles
 All roles with access to MEDS Extractor. 
## Advantages
 The multi-step export job creation workflow simplifies  data entry now that export jobs can pull data from a wider range of sources. The workflow also provides more options for the automatic naming of job files.
## User Impact
 The multistep export job creation workflow appears when you click New Export Job.  Canceling out of creating a job warns you that the export job will not be created.
## Special Considerations
 None.
